- **check-analogy-novelty**: Cross-references each proposed analogy in a 5-framing set against substacker shared-context/analogy-catalog.md to flag reuse. Prevents the writer from recycling "imagine a library" for the twentieth time. Use after generate-analogy-set and before presenting framings to the writer. Trigger keywords — novelty, catalog, analogy reuse, already used, imagine a library. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/tree/HEAD/skills/check-analogy-novelty) · ★200)
